# Universe24: the one run

This document stands on the branch engine-5.0-test, the engine 5.0 under test (the Boss's word of the owner, 2026-10-25, 16:3x UTC): main is 4.0, the tree of 0fcc0403d (2026-10-24, 10:12 UTC, the trains and the parameters declared, where Universe24 ran), returned by the owner's word of 2026-10-25 (15:2x UTC); main's documents are 4.0's and are not edited; the four documents are kept on the branch alone, where the generic Clock's program continues under test, nothing on it a release. This document is the run's: what Universe24 is, what its one run must show, the gates the owner approved, the chart's verdict on each gate by name, what is open, and the commands that run and read the chart. It is one of the project's four documents (the owner's word of 2026-10-25, 10:0x UTC): [docs/HIGHLIGHTS.md](HIGHLIGHTS.md) holds the postulates and the decisions in force, [docs/ALGEBRA.md](ALGEBRA.md) the law, [docs/ENGINE.md](ENGINE.md) the engine, and this one the run. Where a line here points at the law, the law's line governs and no formula is restated. Every number of the chart below is read from the chart's logs (`examples/events/universe24/universe24.clicks.jsonl`, the chart; `universe24.eras.jsonl` and `universe24.nodes.jsonl`, the great clock's looks beside it) through the one reckoner's tables (`readings_clicks.json`) and the folder's `notes.md`; none is typed from memory. The chart read is that of main c60804dc7 (PUSH 37, main 7366816f7; 2,400 ticks of 2026-10-24; the byte check by a second run equal through 1,561, the whole second run waived by the owner's word of 2026-10-25, 00:1x UTC). What does not match nature is named, never hidden; nothing is left open without a name.

## 1. What Universe24 is

One world, one universe, one Clock (the project's postulates in one place, the head of docs/ALGEBRA.md, The postulates, since 2026-10-26). The piece is the universe, the great clock's shape (a cubic lattice wrapped on every axis, a torus), the run's length and, since the owner's approval of 2026-10-25 (ALGEBRA.md, **The setting is a list of settings**), the setting as a list of settings in three forms, `{"universe": ..., "shape": [5, 5, 5], "intervals": 2400, "seed": [...]}`; the chart's file of this document holds the first three ("three things and nothing else" to that approval, kept by name; the engine's loader refuses the key `seed` on this branch, the one src line the item changes). The calibre of the engine's chart is three integers and nothing else, `{"den", "node_clock", "quantum_action"}`: den, Gamma the full rate and T the whole piece's action, nature's constants alone (the law since 2026-10-25 adds the gears' pairs as numbers, no number in the code, and derives G at the load from Gamma, K-S1 of 08:2x UTC GitHub's clock, the key `link_unit` of 06:4x to 08:2x kept by name; the conditions on Gamma, T and den the loader's refusals, **The constants of the universe**, ALGEBRA.md, **The Clock**; the engine reads the three, ENGINE.md, section 3); a file naming any other key is refused at load (HIGHLIGHTS.md, the postulates 1, 6 and 8; ALGEBRA.md, **The Clock**; **No train is declared: the trains emerge at the strike**). The law's line of 2026-10-25 (ALGEBRA.md, **The universe holds the gears' pairs as numbers**) puts the gears' pairs [num, den] in the calibre beside the three as constants read once at the load, numbers and no names; the engine of this branch and the chart below read the three integers and derive den's circle at the load (ENGINE.md, sections 2 and 3). The standing rule of the method (the owner's word of that day): the former way is forbidden, trains declared by name, pairs fitted to the great clock or to nature's ratios, holders and reads typed in; what the engine shows is derived, in order, two blind hands, the cold read, the owner's word on the sentence, the code from the agreed derivation, each property with its small test. The loader sets the settings (light at one quantum on every Clock of the torus but the origin, the hole) and reads the three integers and the shape; after that everything happens by itself at the Clocks: every Clock does Rule3 and the same fixed acts from what it reads over one Mesh, and writes a line, a strike, where it reads a whole quantum (ALGEBRA.md, **The Clock**; **The great clock reads itself**; **The bound bob's strike is the Clock's act**). No bob, no detector, no train, no window and no rate is declared; the fittings, the bobs, the trains, the masses, the clock shifts, the swings and the horizon are the reckoner's readings of the strikes (postulates 7 and 9). What is tested is emergence (the owner's word of 2026-10-25, 18:1x UTC): the loader sets one setting, the calibre of numbers, the great clock, the sea of light and the first bobs, and from that setting alone everything runs, the wells, the Meshes' lengthening and shortening, the turns, the swings, the fittings on the Mesh, the bobs, the strikes; nothing is put in by hand after the load; that is the one trial of Universe24 and the nineteen gates are its reading.

The one run is the project's only main run (HIGHLIGHTS.md, The runs and the chart): Universe24's run of its declared ticks through `tools/eras.py` with its three logs, re-recorded whenever the engine changes under it, its pages drawn from the logs alone and its numbers in the folder's notes and in this document, never as a test's pin. The former worlds (the two slits, the big bang and the cycle, the atom, the strikes worlds, Bell and GHZ) stand as frozen records and run nothing (README.md, The former courses, by name).

## 2. What the run must show: the gates

The owner's words of 2026-10-25 (08:0x to 09:0x UTC, through the Boss; HIGHLIGHTS.md, the decision The chart's gates, approved 08:5x UTC): general relativity and the physics of reality must show in the strikes at the reckoner; the chart's gates are the nine phenomena, general relativity by name and the physics of reality, each a reading of the run's logs through the reckoner with no declaration in the loader, each SHOWN or NOT SHOWN by name on every chart. The gates' predicates and the chart's verdicts below are the agreed derivation of two blind hands (G1 and G2, compared cold by the Boss, 2026-10-25, #2187 comment 6392619280), every number their reading of the chart's three logs on c60804dc7; the predicates are an existence, a sign, an order or a monotonicity, never a typed number. "Board's" marks a gate that reads the whole great clock (the eras or the nodes log) and no inside observer; the others read the strikes (the proper, the position, the readings). The gates stand in code on this branch: `tools/record_gates.py` (`PYTHONPATH=src python tools/record_gates.py examples/events/universe24`, the folder positional), one function per gate, `gate_<name>(record)`, returning SHOWN or NOT SHOWN with its numbers, the great clock's view named, no typed threshold; and tests/test_the_record_gates.py asserting the SHOWN set and the NOT SHOWN set by name on the committed logs. Run on the chart of c60804dc7 the tool reads 13 of 23 verdicts SHOWN, the four gates with two verdicts counted twice (light's presence and its redshift; the horizon's Meshes and silence and its stopped clock; the conservation's identity and its exactness; the mass ratios' fit and their reading from the strikes), the same verdicts as the table below. The nineteen are the list the toy's chart is read against, filled by tools/record_gates.py's verdicts and never by hand, no gate dropped and none added without the owner's word; the fit to nature is as close as the integers allow (the mass ratios to 10^-7 at the 54-bit den) and never nature's precision, said plainly here. A verdict of this document changes only on the Boss's request, from a table or a log. Since 11:0x UTC (the owner's word of 2026-10-25, 11:0x UTC, through the Boss, #2187 comment 6396810268, verbatim in translation: "everything you wrote that did not come out must enter the 19 gates; everything is to be seen there; so the order of the checks is one: you check on a Node, you check on the toy, and then it is to be seen in Universe24 too, so that in Universe24 there is no surprise that things do not happen"): everything not shown enters the gates, every item named NOT SHOWN or a finding against nature by name in the comparisons a gate of the table with its predicate and no note (the exact take, the bobs from born pairs, the collision, free fall, the horizon's stopped clock, the redshift of light, Coulomb's sign, the momentum across the strike, the unspringing, the spinor's two beats and the split, the exclusion, the black body, the exponential decay, light on light, the x polarisation, the Casimir force, the running alpha, the neutron's three-fitted-hand list, the neutrino, and every other such line; the gates of the phenomena, the table below, 20 to 52 by the numbering of that day and the Reader's rows since), and the order of the checks is one: the Clock, then the toy, then Universe24; every gate has its one-Clock test where one Clock can show it, then its reading on the toy's chart (the chiral emergence setting and the bobs' setting, over the cycles), and only then its reading on Universe24's chart, which confirms what the toy showed and names what it did not; David's reckoner reads the same gates(run) on both charts, and the Boss runs both. A derived gate is a prediction, and the chart must show it (the owner's word of 2026-10-25, 11:0x UTC, through the Boss, #2187 comment 6396841814, verbatim in translation: "everything that was derived and is in the rest must happen when you check; no, there are things that are not on a Node, but those things have to be checked from outside, and they must come out in the universes; they are derived and you see them beforehand"): everything derived, the comparisons' sentences and their measured signatures, is written as a gate before the chart is run, blind, with its predicate, the numbers expected and the swing it may need; what one Clock can show is a one-Clock test, what one Clock cannot show (a bob's fall, a collision, the swings, the entropy's rise, the twist's 24 and 24) is read from outside by the reckoner over the chart's credit lines; and in the Universe runs every derived gate must come out. A derived gate NOT SHOWN on the chart is a failure to root-cause, in the engine or in the derivation, named by name and taken back to two hands, never a note left standing; the derivations are seen beforehand, and the chart confirms them or the law is wrong where it says so. The chart tells what it sees as it sees it (the owner's word of 2026-10-25, 11:3x UTC, through the Boss, #2187 comment 6397100411, verbatim in translation: "when there is something beautiful in the run, the decoder should write right away that this happened in the run; in effect there is a visualizer too, no? one that shows the run as an animation"): at the tick a gate first SHOWS, the reckoner writes one line into the chart's log naming the gate and its numbers, the chart's own reading named in the table; and the page (4.0's tools/look/page.py, the torus page) reads for 5.0 the reckoner's output, the great clock's load and the credits per tick as an animation of the torus with the gates' lines as they fire; both after the fold, by the Boss's order (6397100411). Read beside the gates 7, 10, 11 and 12 since 12:5x UTC (the hands PC1 and PC2, ALGEBRA.md, K-S4): by the letter light is the taken at every strike and the taker at none, with no credit line of its own, and the sea's light has no period, so these gates are NOT SHOWN by name until a light hand with a period stands; the 130-bit transient of the chiral setting is the frozen Clock's volume written past the edge and not the clock's, the hands bh1 and bh2 deriving what the frozen clock stops.

### The reckoner's principles (the owner's words of 2026-10-25, 07:2x, 08:0x and 08:3x UTC; ALGEBRA.md, The Clock, the lines of that day)

- The reckoner reads only the Clock's clock and the position, with the readings the credit line carries (the point, the kind, the sense, the support, the beat, the identity, the momentum, the proper ticks), as an observer in nature does; it never reads the great clock's tick as an observer's quantity.
- The reckoner stands at a place: a detector Clock reading its own credits over its own clock and inferring the rest; two detectors share a clock only through a signal of one Mesh per tick.
- The reckoner keeps two views apart (the owner's word of 2026-10-25, 18:0x UTC): the whole view, every strike on the great clock, which nature does not have, the reader's table; and the one-place view, the owner's, where the reckoner finds what a bound bob is from the strikes alone (a Clock, or a set of Clocks within one Mesh, credited every period with its own beat) and reads that bob's clock, its proper ticks, everything of nature (the dilation, the redshift, the Meshes' lengthening) read from one bob's clock against another's; the chart's tables carry both views and the gates of general relativity are read in the second. The reckoner reads in integers (18:1x UTC): every reading a quotient with its remainder, a bit length or a count, never a float.
- The whole-great-clock log is the great clock's view, kept for the gates of the engine and the rewind, and no observer's: a reading made on the eras or the nodes log (the volume's notch U, the mean Mesh e^U, the horizon's Meshes, the Clocks turned) is a look beside the strikes and no measurement, and a gate that rests on it is marked great clock's below.
- All physics is in the reckoner (`tools/clicks_read.py` with `tools/look/captions.py`, nothing of it in `src/`): den's circle, the species and their names, the masses, the bobs and the wells are its readings over several Clocks and several ticks; what does not show in the strikes is not in the world.
- What the lattice conserves is shown conserved in the strikes (the owner's word of 2026-10-25, 06:2x UTC GitHub's clock, through the Boss; ALGEBRA.md, The Clock, **The way to a solution**): a reading the gates carry, from the credit lines alone: the charge as the senses' sum over the fittings (gate 17), the count as taken equals credited (gate 15), the momentum as the credits' running cosine, the energy as the beat per proper tick before and after a strike, the symmetry as the strikes' images under the 48; a solution that conserves on the great clock and not in the strikes is not shown. On this chart gates 15 and 17 carry it; the momentum, the energy and the 48 in the strikes are readings the reckoner is to land by the method (tools/record_gates.py), named here as the lack.

### The chart of the merged engine (2026-10-25, 13:1x UTC, the Boss's run; engine-5.0-test d752d586f)

The first long chart on the merged engine, tests/worlds/world_chiral.json (world_nature.json since 2026-10-26, 04:4x UTC, the owner's word of 03:5x) at 2,400 ticks on the 5-cube (74 seconds), run by the Boss's own hand (#2187 comment 6398046061): every integer and transient within 128 bits (the state 115, the largest transient 108), 98 integers per Clock constant, the rewind exact 10 of 10, the charge 0 at every one of 14 fittings, the entropy of the strikes 1,022 bits never falling, the mass ratios of the toy's den read from the decoded points (gapped 1 over gapped 0 at 1,836,153,562 per 10^9). The fold made the symmetric ride the tick's form (the owner's sentence (1)), faithful to the toy with its ride key on (fittings 14, bob strikes 0, the refusals 361 and 28 alike); under the ride the pump is gone (R4's finding), and with it on these settings: bob credits 0 where the morning's pumped chart had 311, the era of matter NOT SHOWN, the horizon NOT REACHED (the max load 2,247 against the frozen 3,793; the collapse world of 2N = 800 on the 9-cube 1,185 at 400), no top and no swing in 2,400 (the volume's mean 1,002 at 1, 39,099 at 100, a dip to 25,690 at 200, about 10^6 by 1,400), the fittings all within the first 11 ticks; the morning's 130-bit transient was the pumped chart's frozen Clock and does not arise. So the gates of matter, of gravity's collapse and of the swings wait on the sentences the hands W, G, V and OB named as owed, which the pump was carrying: V-D (the vacuum's weight for the counts), W-S1 (the emission), the momentum across the strike (the sentence (3)), G-S4 (what sustains a bob's well at the law's letter) and the unspringing (OB-S3); the hands bh1 and bh2 are redirected to the engine's default (whether the horizon is reached at all without the pump, then the frozen Clock's step and drive). The morning's SHOWN rows on the pumped toy are kept by name as that chart's.

### The gates' ledger on the current head (the owner's word of 2026-10-25, 14:3x UTC by the Boss's clock, through the Boss, #2187 comment 6398664921; kept by the Scribe on every merge from the Boss's and Animate's tables)

The owner's word, verbatim in translation: "all the time make sure the things are seen in Universe24; every gate must be seen happening; if they do not happen there is a problem; everything from the seed." The Boss's standing duty from it (THE_WAY widened, BRIEF_COMMON's Addition 9): after every merge the Boss runs the chart from the setting alone and reads every gate; a gate NOT SHOWN is a problem named with the item that owes it. The count is read against every row of this section, the nineteen and the widened 20 to 54 (the owner's "there are more than 19" of 14:1x UTC and his goal of 15:2x, "to bring Universe24 as close as possible to 50"): SHOWN in Universe24, N of 54, the Boss's count (6399612901); Animate, the Reader, counts N of 50, the rows 29, 30, 31 and 33 read once with 11, 10, 12 and 13 whose predicates they restate. Universe24 is the chiral setting to 2,400 from the setting alone, nothing fed; the side worlds (collapse_800 to 600, bobs to 300, hole to 900) are the reader's view beside it. ### The gates widened to everything derived, read on any swing of the same setting and at one Clock where one Clock can show it (2026-10-25, 11:0x UTC)

The owner's two words (the owner's word of 2026-10-25, 11:0x UTC, through the Boss, #2187 comment 6396795317, verbatim in translation: "also everything you derive and need to see ... everything that was derived and seen, that you are supposed to see as nature does, add it to the gates, because you want to see not the 19, you want to see everything that was derived, you want to see it on the board from the same seed; you need not see it in one bang, you can see it in the second, the third, the fourth, the fifth bang ... but you can also check that they are seen at one Node"; and the owner's word of 2026-10-25, 11:0x UTC, through the Boss, #2187 comment 6396810268, verbatim in translation: "everything you wrote that did not come out must enter the 19 gates; everything is to be seen there; so the order of the checks is one: you check on a Node, you check on the toy, and then it is to be seen in Universe24 too, so that in Universe24 there is no surprise that things do not happen"): every derivation that says what nature's strikes show is a gate, read by the reckoner from the chart of the same setting (the chiral emergence setting and the bobs' setting) on any swing of the run, a gate SHOWN on the third swing SHOWN, and each also a one-Clock test where one Clock can show it; every item named NOT SHOWN or a finding against nature by name in the comparisons under tools/derivations/open_items is a gate of this table with its predicate and no note, SHOWN or NOT SHOWN on the chart by the reckoner, nothing left out because the law lacks it. The order of the checks is one: the Clock, then the toy, then Universe24; a gate that did not show on the toy is not expected on Universe24. ### The properties the engine shows, each with its test

The owner's word of 2026-10-25 (11:2x UTC, through the Boss, #2187 comment 6392645854): every property the Clock showed with the trains' trains keeps working under the generic Clock, the Meshes lengthening, the reversed clock, gravity and all the rest, each with a small test showing the property exists (an existence, a sign, an order or a monotonicity; no typed course number; under 15 seconds), the list written here as the gate of every stage of the generic Clock, the chart's gates of the table above beside it. The inventory is the agreed derivation of two blind hands (P1, 60 properties; P2, 46 at a coarser grain; compared cold by the Boss, #2187 comment 6392787098): every property the engine shows on main has a test asserting its existence except about ten, the same ten in both lists; 17 to 19 properties lean on the trains' trains and each has its re-posing for the generic Clock (the same statement read from the fixed gears or from the reckoner; the lean great clock's equality retiring into the count gate; the selector, den's circle and the bob's identity moving whole to the reckoner; the one sign train written by the Mesh's fitting the open derivation). The sixty lines below are the agreed inventory in the owner's order (the Boss's post of 2026-10-25, #2187 comment 6392886380; P2's eight groups head P1's finer lines), each with its test in the tree (file::function, mapped by the Scribe from the tests' docstrings for the Boss's cold read) or the test to land; a line that leans on the trains' trains carries its re-posing for the generic Clock in one clause (the hands' column: the same statement read from the fixed gears or from the reckoner). The ten tests named to land stand on this branch in tests/test_the_properties_hold_on_a_run.py (twelve functions on the bare 3-cube at den 1,021 under the hole setting, each an existence, a sign, an order or a monotonicity derived from the law's integers), beside the one-Clock toys (tests/test_the_generic_node_toy.py and tests/test_the_generic_node_toys_close_and_constants.py, seventeen tests of the Clock's acts and the owner's three constants at one Clock fed through its six Meshes, calibrated bit for bit against the great clock) and the chart's gates (tests/test_the_record_gates.py); the suite on the branch 123 functions in 45 files. Beside the list, the properties of nature the engine does not show stand by name in section 4 and in FURTHER_RESEARCH.md's first section.

The Meshes lengthening:

1. The Meshes lengthen as the load deepens: X rises under the light's drive, e^U rises above 1, the clock and the mesh rate fall (**The volume holder's rest is the clock's one notch**; **The rates compose**). tests/test_the_properties_hold_on_a_run.py::test_the_links_lengthen_on_the_running_board.
2. The lengthening is read in the strikes: the spacing of a Clock's credits is longer where e^U is larger (**The strike's view: the readings on the credit line**; the gate 2). tests/test_the_properties_hold_on_a_run.py::test_the_lengthening_is_read_in_the_clicks; the chart's reading in tests/test_the_readers_tables.py::test_the_seven_tables_read_the_committed_record_through_the_command_line.

The reversed clock:

3. The turnover: a Clock at a weak maximum of its volume's time gear ends the tick with its notches exchanged and its remainders complemented, an involution; the floor turns the same way; the massless pair never turns (**The turnover**; **The floor**). tests/test_the_turn_at_the_floor.py::test_a_top_turns_every_node and ::test_the_floor_turns_as_the_top_does; tests/test_the_acts_table.py::test_the_fifth_act_the_swap_at_the_top_divides_nothing_and_is_its_own_inverse.
4. On the course a Clock turns at its own top; the light's velocity flips there; the rewind through the turn is exact (**The turnover**; **The inverse**). tests/test_the_properties_hold_on_a_run.py::test_the_clock_reverses_at_a_nodes_own_top_on_the_course.
5. Ten thousand phase acts forward and back return the setting bit for bit (**The sign holder rotates the two-part hand**). tests/test_the_phase_line.py::test_the_reversal_is_bit_exact_over_ten_thousand_acts.

Gravity:

6. Gravity: a hand's form sources the volume at its Clock and nowhere beyond one Mesh; X rises there and the clock slows there (**The volume holder's rest is the clock's one notch**; **Only local**). tests/test_the_properties_hold_on_a_run.py::test_gravitys_sign_on_two_boards.
7. The dilation of the proper clock: the deeper Clock counts fewer proper ticks (**The rates compose**; the gate 10). tests/test_the_properties_hold_on_a_run.py::test_the_dilations_sign_by_hand.

## 3. The chart of c60804dc7 (PUSH 37) in brief

The run of 2,400 ticks of the 5-cube at the PeV den, 21,397,363,965,165,989,151,899,244,775,347,635,252,768,274,397 = 8,909,066,857,637 x 79,961,009,336,473 x 30,036,532,038,439,967,297 (the header: the width 225 bits, Gamma 1,000, T 36,000, 29 rows), run once by the README's command in 3,753.6 seconds (1.564 per tick, the largest 2.283 at 706). The strikes log holds 22,014 lines: 11,975 credits (54 light's at the fittings, 11,921 the bound bobs' from 21 to 2,394), 54 fittings and 9,985 settings. The swing at 1 (X 1,000 at the origin, 1,002 at the other 124 Clocks, the first fire at 1, six Clocks turned at 2, 56 at 8); the fittings at 12 to 17, 54 pairs at 47 Clocks (7 proton, 14 electron, 33 neutron pairs at the senses -1 and 1, the sum 0), none after; the bobs' strikes every period, every hand at every one of the 125 Clocks over the run; the tops at 425 and 1,360, the returns at 601 and 1,530 (the reckoner's swings 2 and 3, with no fitting), U 2.4807 at 2,400 and rising; 1,495 Clock turns, 70 Clocks turned at 2,400, no turn at the floor; the horizon from 1,524 in three black holes, the most 78 Meshes at the teeth count at 2,134, 12 at 2,400; the light not spent, 26,183,034 quanta at 2,400. Read wrong by the reckoner, named and not patched: a bob credit's point and momentum in the bobs and uncertainty tables (notes.md, What the reader misreads); the six checks read neither. The chart was run at the PeV den before the owner's word of 2026-10-25 (04:1x and 07:2x UTC) bounding den by the 128 bits: the next chart runs at the den of the hand 3,311,818,282 (K-S3, the owner's word of 08:2x UTC GitHub's clock, even; G-S1's 1,655,909,141 of 07:1x and the top tier's den 1,166,991,900,949,580,266,237 before it, kept by name), 697,786 for cheap runs, with Gamma the largest odd within the width there (HIGHLIGHTS.md, The den), and this document's verdicts are re-read on it. The committed chart is the half-up engine's output (the owner's word of 2026-10-25, 13:5x UTC through the Boss, the past with tick 23 does not interest him): under the odd rounding of signed coefficients (ALGEBRA.md, the turn's factor and the drive's factor, the clauses of 2026-10-25) its eras and nodes logs would part from the engine at tick 23 (16 turn ties at tick 21, L = +-20 and p_0 = 775 at the eight central Clocks, the Boss's hands R1 and R2), the strikes log equal through 30; the chart stands by name as it is, and the next chart, at the den within the 128 bits, is the engine's.

## 4. What is open, by name
### The ledger of 2026-10-25, 13:2x UTC, after the fold (the owner's question, "do you have a ledger of what is open and what is not?"; kept current by the Scribe on every push and every word)

**The Scribe's method** (the owner's word of 2026-10-25, 15:4x UTC in the session, verbatim in translation: "whatever seems right to you, establish a working method"; standing): (1) every word of the owner is recorded verbatim and dated and enters the documents within the hour, a decision line in HIGHLIGHTS.md, the law's sentence in ALGEBRA.md with the former clause kept by name, its row in this ledger, one line in CHANGELOG.md; since the owner's word of 23:59 UTC through the Boss's lines (6405196295, "make the work efficient, for everyone"): one commit per hour of words and not one per word, one merge of engine-5.0-test per hour, the gates once per commit, the open table's post once per hour unless the Boss asks, every post ending with the goal line (the recurring swing's state and the gates SHOWN on nature's chart, the owner's standing order of 05:4x UTC), the thread read by the Boss's pokes with the comment ids and not by pages, and no run of the suite by the Scribe; from 03:1x UTC the conservation kit run once per commit on the Clock world and the chiral, its lines recorded in row 170; from 06:0x UTC (the owner's word, in translation: "when there is a unification or something closes, make sure there are no more calls in the code to the things that were said to be unified; something generic; when you read the code that is pushed you must check these things") every push read for the leftovers of what it unifies or closes: no live call, branch, key or name of the former separate forms stays in the acts (the former spelling lives in a docstring as kept by name and nowhere else), no clause that says "to be unified" or "until X lands" stays once X landed, no act of a closed item stays reachable; a leftover named to the Boss as a departure before the merge, by the unification's name (one full check per item by the one who pushes, the Boss's at the merge; the Scribe reads every push whole for the five refusals and locality and takes the numbers from the push's line, the confirming hand and the Boss's chart cache); the former cadence, one commit per word and the Scribe's own run of every push, kept by name; (2) every push is read whole and run before the Boss's merge, the five refusals first, then locality, then the five conservation checks with numbers, a breach named to the Boss with its commit, file, line and the law it crosses; (3) the law leads the engine: an approved sentence enters ALGEBRA.md as "enters as built" and is marked built with its commit when the push lands and a hand that did not derive it confirms, ENGINE.md's list gaining its row and its test then; (4) a section that describes code no longer in the tree is re-cut by the Scribe to the tree of the head, the former kept by name in one dated line, and no law sentence changes without the owner's word; by the owner's word of 23:1x UTC ("go for it", on the Scribe's proposal of the same minute) the law itself is re-cut to the head section by section beside the engine's cleaning, every formula once in its final form with the short derivation from the hands by name, the superseded clauses moved as written to ALGEBRA.md's closing section The former clauses, kept by name, The Clock and the sign train's drive first; (5) the gates' ledger on the current head is the Boss's and the Reader's table and nothing the Scribe reads on its own, a gate with no reading on the head named as not read; (6) the current letter names a train by its shape and pair, the former wording stays as written and dated; ## 5. The commands

The chart's run, the reckoner, the two pages and the rewind, from a checkout's root with the package installed (Python 3.14 or later; README.md, Run it):

```
PYTHONPATH=src nice -n 10 python tools/eras.py examples/events/universe24/universe24.json --every 100 --span 50 --out examples/events/universe24/universe24.eras.jsonl --clicks examples/events/universe24/universe24.clicks.jsonl --nodes examples/events/universe24/universe24.nodes.jsonl
PYTHONPATH=src python tools/clicks_read.py examples/events/universe24/universe24.clicks.jsonl --shape 5 5 5 --eras examples/events/universe24/universe24.eras.jsonl --nodes examples/events/universe24/universe24.nodes.jsonl --out examples/events/universe24/readings_clicks.json
PYTHONPATH=src python tools/look/eras_page.py examples/events/universe24/universe24.eras.jsonl --clicks examples/events/universe24/universe24.clicks.jsonl --nodes examples/events/universe24/universe24.nodes.jsonl --notes examples/events/universe24/notes.md --out examples/events/universe24/universe24.html
PYTHONPATH=src python tools/look/torus_page.py examples/events/universe24/universe24.nodes.jsonl --eras examples/events/universe24/universe24.eras.jsonl --clicks examples/events/universe24/universe24.clicks.jsonl --world examples/events/universe24/universe24.json --notes examples/events/universe24/notes.md --out examples/events/universe24/universe24.torus.html
PYTHONPATH=src python tools/back_in_time.py examples/events/universe24/universe24.json --from K --intervals N
PYTHONPATH=src python tools/node_integers.py examples/events/universe24/universe24.json --intervals 13 --bits 128
```

The strikes log is the chart; the eras and the nodes logs are looks beside it. The tests that touch the chart: tests/test_universe24_runs_its_cycle.py (the bare file loads; the small bare torus is seeded with the hole, swings and fittings its pairs), tests/test_the_readers_tables.py (the reckoner's tables by hand and the smoke check of the thirteen tables on the committed chart), tests/test_a_click_does_what_it_should.py (the owner's standing test of the strike). The check before every landing: `PYTHONPATH=src python tools/check.py --base main`.
